# Skills · W · part 92

- **write-skill**: Use when creating a skill in .claude/skills/, editing a SKILL.md, turning a postmortem into a skill, or when a skill doesn't trigger or isn't followed. Covers the trigger description, steps, examples, anti-patterns, the size limit, and checking a skill with its evals. - **write-skill**: Authoring standard for new skills or substantive skill revisions. Covers role taxonomy, required sections, size targets, the B-13 skill/guide compression pattern, and a pre-save quality checklist — everything `capability-hygiene` domain enforces. ([KeenanHoffman/NeuroGrim](https://github.com/KeenanHoffman/NeuroGrim/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/write-skill) · ★1 · 2 copies)
